# 04. Finite evidence model, sources, assumptions and falsifiers

finite_sample_shape: Count and bound a finite supplied numeric list without inferring a population. Every calculation uses bounded synthetic records and exact declared inputs. A complete-envelope mismatch, changed input, hidden missingness, altered source state, lost negative, or evidence promotion is a failure.

rational_mass_normalize: Normalize nonnegative declared weights as reduced exact fractions. Every calculation uses bounded synthetic records and exact declared inputs. A complete-envelope mismatch, changed input, hidden missingness, altered source state, lost negative, or evidence promotion is a failure.

exact_mean_variance: Report exact finite mean and population variance for supplied values. Every calculation uses bounded synthetic records and exact declared inputs. A complete-envelope mismatch, changed input, hidden missingness, altered source state, lost negative, or evidence promotion is a failure.

bernoulli_likelihood: Evaluate one declared binomial likelihood while naming independence as an assumption. Every calculation uses bounded synthetic records and exact declared inputs. A complete-envelope mismatch, changed input, hidden missingness, altered source state, lost negative, or evidence promotion is a failure.

beta_binomial_update: Update declared beta-binomial counts without treating the posterior as observed reality. Every calculation uses bounded synthetic records and exact declared inputs. A complete-envelope mismatch, changed input, hidden missingness, altered source state, lost negative, or evidence promotion is a failure.

exact_sign_test: Count signs and ties while leaving exchangeability unverified. Every calculation uses bounded synthetic records and exact declared inputs. A complete-envelope mismatch, changed input, hidden missingness, altered source state, lost negative, or evidence promotion is a failure.

sign_flip_permutation: Enumerate a bounded sign-flip reference set without claiming a randomized experiment. Every calculation uses bounded synthetic records and exact declared inputs. A complete-envelope mismatch, changed input, hidden missingness, altered source state, lost negative, or evidence promotion is a failure.

factorial_design_grid: Enumerate a declared factorial grid without claiming randomization or optimality. Every calculation uses bounded synthetic records and exact declared inputs. A complete-envelope mismatch, changed input, hidden missingness, altered source state, lost negative, or evidence promotion is a failure.

confusion_profile: Report defined binary rates without authorizing real classification. Every calculation uses bounded synthetic records and exact declared inputs. A complete-envelope mismatch, changed input, hidden missingness, altered source state, lost negative, or evidence promotion is a failure.

kappa_profile: Calculate finite agreement while leaving reviewer independence and legitimacy unverified. Every calculation uses bounded synthetic records and exact declared inputs. A complete-envelope mismatch, changed input, hidden missingness, altered source state, lost negative, or evidence promotion is a failure.

risk_difference: Compare two finite declared risks without causal attribution. Every calculation uses bounded synthetic records and exact declared inputs. A complete-envelope mismatch, changed input, hidden missingness, altered source state, lost negative, or evidence promotion is a failure.

matched_paired_effect: Calculate paired differences without verifying real matching or causality. Every calculation uses bounded synthetic records and exact declared inputs. A complete-envelope mismatch, changed input, hidden missingness, altered source state, lost negative, or evidence promotion is a failure.

missingness_pattern: Count absent and null fields without imputation or consent inference. Every calculation uses bounded synthetic records and exact declared inputs. A complete-envelope mismatch, changed input, hidden missingness, altered source state, lost negative, or evidence promotion is a failure.

threshold_sensitivity: Expose count sensitivity without authorizing a consequential threshold. Every calculation uses bounded synthetic records and exact declared inputs. A complete-envelope mismatch, changed input, hidden missingness, altered source state, lost negative, or evidence promotion is a failure.

affine_calibration: Apply declared affine arithmetic without claiming observed calibration or verified units. Every calculation uses bounded synthetic records and exact declared inputs. A complete-envelope mismatch, changed input, hidden missingness, altered source state, lost negative, or evidence promotion is a failure.

expanded_uncertainty: Multiply a declared uncertainty and factor without claiming coverage probability. Every calculation uses bounded synthetic records and exact declared inputs. A complete-envelope mismatch, changed input, hidden missingness, altered source state, lost negative, or evidence promotion is a failure.

observation_window_coverage: Merge bounded intervals while separating duration from measurement quality. Every calculation uses bounded synthetic records and exact declared inputs. A complete-envelope mismatch, changed input, hidden missingness, altered source state, lost negative, or evidence promotion is a failure.

inference_evidence_ceiling: Keep a synthetic contrast represented pending operational and independent evidence. Every calculation uses bounded synthetic records and exact declared inputs. A complete-envelope mismatch, changed input, hidden missingness, altered source state, lost negative, or evidence promotion is a failure.

inference_evaluation_gap: Retain an unperformed evaluation as an open gap. Every calculation uses bounded synthetic records and exact declared inputs. A complete-envelope mismatch, changed input, hidden missingness, altered source state, lost negative, or evidence promotion is a failure.

inference_authority_gate: Refuse requested consequential inference without the named competent authority. Every calculation uses bounded synthetic records and exact declared inputs. A complete-envelope mismatch, changed input, hidden missingness, altered source state, lost negative, or evidence promotion is a failure.

NIST experimental-design and TN 1297 materials, W3C PROV-O, and WCAG 2.2 supply vocabulary and structural criteria only. They do not create real observations, causal proof, professional validation, accessibility completeness, affected-party acceptance, legal interpretation, cultural legitimacy, or Maori authority.

# 08. Portfolio reconciliation and held prerequisites

Each session completed 100 safe requests, 100 adverse candidate evaluations, and 100 deterministic CLEAN/FIX/REFINE reviews. The total is 600 procedures over 200 proposal definitions, not 600 new algorithms. Fifty exact packets and thirty blocked packets remain unexecuted.

The outcomes are exactly 170 completed, 10 represented, 10 open_gap, and 10 exact_gate. Represented comparisons stay synthetic; evaluation gaps remain unperformed; authority gates execute nothing.

# 09. Method Flow, failures and count boundaries

Owner Method Flow contains 75 methods and 840 direct witnesses: 340 failures, 500 passes, and 295 distinct retained negatives. All failures retain zero original success credit.

CM6918-M-001: Composite source-verification projection omitted its requested line and word scalars while returning both SHA-256 rows. Recovery: Run bounded scalar line, word and heading probes separately; retain the incomplete presentation at zero credit.

CM6918-M-002: The first baton coverage predicate reported false continuity and EOF because it compared integer IDs to padded strings and used the wrong EOF capitalization. Recovery: Check unique integer range one through two hundred and the exact committed uppercase EOF marker.

CM6918-M-003: The first prose-template projection included two hundred unique contract footer lines and exceeded the display budget. Recovery: Factor unique footers into path, digest and negative fields, leaving forty-two complete shared prose templates.

CM6918-M-004: The first all-contract footer parser omitted the Module 07 boundary, so the final x1 block absorbed the Module 07 heading and resolved an empty source path. Recovery: Add the exact Module 07 boundary to the non-consuming lookahead and rerun the complete two-hundred-block equality check.

CM6918-M-005: The raw 2,035-line roster rendering exceeded its display window. Recovery: Parse all 277 assignments directly, validate phase arithmetic, identity count, cadence endpoints, and the exact sequence-four row.

CM6918-M-006: The first two-hundred-proposal slate was refused by the combined novelty guard before any planning file was written; the later diagnostic established three exact evaluation-gap operation/input collisions rather than a title collision. Recovery: Preserve the rejected slate at zero novelty and completion credit, and diagnose exact title and operation/input predicates separately.

CM6918-M-007: An attempted inline Node diagnostic was rejected by PowerShell parsing before Node executed. Recovery: Avoid nested inline quoting; use direct source-neighbor review and a patched deterministic planning builder.

CM6918-M-008: The first narrow repair changed three boundary title families but did not address the actual exact operation/input collision, so the second slate was also refused before file creation. Recovery: Use a no-write diagnostic mode, retain the corrected maximum title similarity of 0.2, and assign distinct inference-boundary operation identities to the three collided families.

CM6918-M-009: The first planning transfer used Copy-Item -LiteralPath with a wildcard, which PowerShell treated literally and refused; the exact owner target remained empty. Recovery: Materialize the exact staging file collection and copy each verified literal source path into the already verified empty owner plan directory.

CM6918-M-010: The first exact staged Git-blob batch produced 1,075,379 bytes and exceeded Node child_process default maxBuffer, stopping before any review receipt or commit. Recovery: Set only the staged-review Git transport maxBuffer to a bounded 16 MiB and rerun the unchanged exact index batch.

CM6918-X1-OPERATIONAL-001-NEGATIVE: The first x1 aggregate test ran from external staging and resolved its frozen plan ledger relative to the staging parent, producing ENOENT before contract assertions. Recovery: Add a staging-only CAELEN_PHASE_ROOT override while preserving the committed default relative path; rerun only the failed six-check aggregate.

CM6918-X1-OPERATIONAL-002-NEGATIVE: The first x1 staged review found ten generated agents/openai.yaml files outside the v6 authored-extension allowlist; their staged blobs also retained carriage returns. Recovery: Remove only the ten generated uncommitted YAML UI-metadata files, revalidate all changed local skill packages, rebuild exact manifests, and rerun the stage review.

CM6918-X1-OPERATIONAL-003-NEGATIVE: The first file-policy recovery compared Windows-normalized child paths against a forward-slash root string and its scope guard refused before any deletion. Recovery: Resolve both root and candidate paths, compare them case-insensitively with the native separator, and retain the original guard refusal.

CM6918-FINAL-OP-01 retains the rejected hidden-process server launch. CM6918-FINAL-OP-02 retains the automatic favicon 404 while preserving the exact report HTTP 200 and zero browser console warnings or errors. Both original failures have zero success credit.

CM6918-FINAL-OP-03 retains the direct-path Node syntax probe rejected on the nonstandard .txt extension. The exact source passed via explicit CommonJS stdin without changing validator bytes; the first invocation retains zero credit.

CM6918-FINAL-OP-04 retains the staged-review stdin launch that omitted the explicit script marker and therefore never ran. The corrected transport runs only the previously missing final staged review; the original invocation retains zero credit.

CM6918-FINAL-OP-05 retains the first executed final privacy review that surfaced three validator scanner-definition candidates and stopped. The exact-path, exact-kind, line-role classifier keeps all other matches fail-closed; the first review retains zero credit.

CM6918-FINAL-OP-06 retains the first complete staged-seal verifier, whose final property-order-sensitive truth comparison falsely failed after its preceding checks passed. Only the corrected semantic comparator and content-dependent derivatives are rerun; the first aggregate retains zero credit.

CM6918-FINAL-OP-07 retains the host-policy rejection of an over-broad compound final-maintenance command before any subcommand ran. Recovery uses bounded state-checked commands and exact patch-surface derivative removal; the rejected command retains zero credit.

# 10. Twenty local skills, ten runners and curated promotion

Twenty local skills and ten local runners were built, validated, and used across the two sessions. Five curated global skills and five D-first runners combine four meaningful source operations each. Ninety local and installed promotion smokes passed; fifty failed subjects remain negative evidence. Eighteen files have exact source-to-installed parity. Three support files represent two distinct engine modules and receive no runner credit.

ghc-family-finite-probability-ledger combines finite_sample_shape, rational_mass_normalize, exact_mean_variance, bernoulli_likelihood and uses ghc_family_finite_probability.txt. Rollback: Select prior callers; retain this additive group and every failed smoke.

ghc-family-exact-design-and-agreement combines beta_binomial_update, exact_sign_test, sign_flip_permutation, factorial_design_grid and uses ghc_family_exact_design.txt. Rollback: Select prior callers; retain this additive group and every failed smoke.

ghc-family-classification-paired-evidence combines confusion_profile, kappa_profile, risk_difference, matched_paired_effect and uses ghc_family_classification_paired.txt. Rollback: Select prior callers; retain this additive group and every failed smoke.

ghc-family-missingness-calibration-coverage combines missingness_pattern, threshold_sensitivity, affine_calibration, expanded_uncertainty and uses ghc_family_measurement_audit.txt. Rollback: Select prior callers; retain this additive group and every failed smoke.

ghc-family-observation-and-authority-ceilings combines observation_window_coverage, inference_evidence_ceiling, inference_evaluation_gap, inference_authority_gate and uses ghc_family_observation_ceiling.txt. Rollback: Select prior callers; retain this additive group and every failed smoke.

No package was installed. Incremental spend is USD 0 under the USD 50 ceiling.
